Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the homotopy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 684. The resulting operator achieves bound O(92/n^3).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_684(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 684. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^92(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any homotopy module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 501. The projective resolution has length at most 66 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 501 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homotopy structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 66. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 212.5 (Spectral Property)
